Result: 成功将许多强化学习经典结果推广到一般状态-动作空间MDP，突破了有限状态-动作空间的限制，并基于此框架开发了新的低复杂度PPO类算法。

Conclusion: 该算子理论框架为一般状态-动作空间MDP提供了统一的理论基础，扩展了强化学习理论适用范围，并启发了新的高效算法设计。

Abstract: Markov decision processes (MDPs) is viewed as an optimization of an objective function over certain linear operators over general function spaces. Using the well-established perturbation theory of linear operators, this viewpoint allows one to identify derivatives of the objective function as a function of the linear operators. This leads to generalization of many well-known results in reinforcement learning to cases with generate state and action spaces. Prior results of this type were only established in the finite-state finite-action MDP settings and in settings with certain linear function approximations. The framework also leads to new low-complexity PPO-type reinforcement learning algorithms for general state and action space MDPs.

</details>


### [189] [RAMP: Reinforcement Adaptive Mixed Precision Quantization for Efficient On Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17891)
*Arpit Singh Gautam,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAMP：基于强化学习的自适应混合精度量化框架，通过分层比特分配优化LLM部署，在保持精度的同时显著减少模型大小


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法通常采用统一的比特宽度，导致精度与效率的权衡不够优化。需要一种能够根据各层特性自适应分配比特宽度的智能方法，以实现更好的压缩效果同时保持模型性能。

Method: 1. 提出RAMP框架：基于离策略Soft Actor-Critic强化学习算法，学习每层比特分配策略以最小化困惑度并满足全局比特预算
2. 策略输入：11维嵌入向量，包含激活统计、权重特性和结构描述符
3. Scale Folding技术：通过每通道缩放和归一化层补偿，将激活异常值迁移到权重中，实现稳定的4比特以下量化
4. 质量优先奖励函数：采用非对称惩罚和预算悬崖机制驱动快速收敛
5. HALO流水线：将分配方案导出为GGUF格式，支持CPU、GPU和边缘设备的无内核推理

Result: 1. Llama 2 7B：在3.68GB（3.65有效比特）下达到5.54困惑度，优于均匀4比特AWQ（5.60困惑度，3.90GB）和GPTQ
2. 尺寸减少6%，质量提升1-3%
3. 零样本泛化：仅在Llama 2 7B上训练的策略能够零样本泛化到Llama 2 13B和Mistral 7B，甚至超过针对特定目标的训练
4. 保持99.5%的FP16常识推理性能

Conclusion: RAMP证明了量化敏感性主要与架构相关而非特定模型，通过强化学习驱动的自适应混合精度量化能够在保持模型性能的同时显著减少存储需求。该方法具有零样本泛化能力，为大规模语言模型在资源受限设备上的高效部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Post training quantization is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) on resource constrained hardware, yet state of the art methods enforce uniform bit widths across layers, yielding suboptimal accuracy efficiency trade offs. We present RAMP (Reinforcement Adaptive Mixed Precision), an off policy Soft Actor Critic framework that learns per layer bit width assignments to minimize perplexity under a global bit budget. The policy conditions on an 11 dimensional embedding of activation statistics, weight properties, and structural descriptors, enabling zero shot transfer across model families and scales. To enable stable sub 4 bit quantization, we introduce Scale Folding, a preconditioning technique that migrates activation outliers into weights via per channel scaling and normalization layer compensation. A quality prioritized reward with asymmetric penalties and budget cliffs drives rapid convergence. On Llama 2 7B, RAMP achieves 5.54 perplexity at 3.68GB (3.65 effective bits), outperforming uniform 4 bit AWQ (5.60 at 3.90 GB) and GPTQ by 6% in size and 1% to3% in quality. Critically, a policy trained only on Llama 2 7B generalizes zero shot to Llama 2 13B and Mistral 7B, often surpassing target specific training, supporting the hypothesis that quantization sensitivity is primarily architectural. The HALO pipeline exports allocations to GGUF format for kernel free inference on CPUs, GPUs, and edge devices, retaining 99.5% of FP16 commonsense reasoning performance.

</details>


### [190] [Only relative ranks matter in weight-clustered large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17917)
*Borja Aizpurua,Sukhbinder Singh,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过权重聚类将LLM的权重矩阵压缩为16-64个共享值，保持相对排序比精确数值更重要，秩保持随机化几乎不影响性能，而秩破坏则导致质量急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型包含数十亿参数，但许多精确值并非必需。研究发现权重相对排序（连接强弱关系）比精确数值更重要，这为训练无关的模型压缩提供了新视角。

Method: 对预训练模型应用权重聚类（K-means），将每个权重矩阵替换为K个共享值；可选微调聚类中心；系统随机化聚类中心同时保持分配固定，分析秩保持与秩破坏随机化的影响；进行逐层替换实验研究尺度漂移机制。

Result: 将每个矩阵压缩到16-64个不同值可保持强准确性；微调聚类中心可恢复30-40%的剩余精度差距；秩破坏随机化导致困惑度数量级增加，而秩保持随机化几乎无损失；逐层替换显示尺度漂移是主要崩溃机制，但仿射校正可显著延迟漂移。

Conclusion: 权重相对排序对LLM性能至关重要，基于秩的视角为模型压缩和鲁棒性提供了新思路，简单的训练无关压缩方法能有效减少存储需求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) contain billions of parameters, yet many exact values are not essential. We show that what matters most is the relative rank of weights-whether one connection is stronger or weaker than another-rather than precise magnitudes. To reduce the number of unique weight values, we apply weight clustering to pretrained models, replacing every weight matrix with K shared values from K-means. For Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct and SmolLM2-135M, reducing each matrix to only 16-64 distinct values preserves strong accuracy without retraining, providing a simple, training-free method to compress LLMs on disk. Optionally fine-tuning only the cluster means (centroids) recovers 30-40 percent of the remaining accuracy gap at minimal cost. We then systematically randomize cluster means while keeping assignments fixed. Scrambling the relative ranks of the clusters degrades quality sharply-perplexity can increase by orders of magnitude-even when global statistics such as mean and variance are preserved. In contrast, rank-preserving randomizations cause almost no loss at mid and late layers. On the other hand, when many layers are perturbed simultaneously, progressive layer-by-layer replacement reveals that scale drift-not rank distortion-is the dominant collapse mechanism; however, an affine correction w' = aw + b with a > 0 (which preserves both rank order and overall weight distribution) can substantially delay this drift. This rank-based perspective offers a new lens on model compression and robustness.

</details>


### [191] [CARE: Covariance-Aware and Rank-Enhanced Decomposition for Enabling Multi-Head Latent Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17946)
*Zhongzhu Zhou,Fengxiang Bie,Ziyan Chen,Zhenyu Zhang,Yibo Yang,Junxiong Wang,Ben Athiwaratkun,Xiaoxia Wu,Shuaiwen Leon Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE提出了一种协方差感知、秩增强的MLA转换方法，通过激活保持分解、调整秩分配和KV奇偶映射，在固定KV宽度下将预训练注意力模块转换为多潜在头注意力，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将预训练注意力模块（如GQA）转换为多潜在头注意力（MLA）的方法存在三个主要问题：1）仅基于权重矩阵差异进行低秩近似，忽略激活影响；2）忽略激活的协方差结构；3）在各层间采用均匀秩分配，导致激活漂移和注意力保真度下降。

Method: CARE包含三个关键步骤：1）激活保持分解：基于实际输入激活而非仅权重进行近似；2）调整秩分配：在固定KV预算下，为需要更多容量的层分配更高秩；3）KV奇偶映射：重新参数化转换后的K和V以适配MLA格式，同时保持KV缓存大小不变。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B/30B-A3B-Instruct-2507和Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct模型上，CARE相比均匀秩SVD基线，单次困惑度降低高达215倍，平均准确率提升高达1.70倍。经过简短的SVD后微调，可完全恢复原始模型准确率。

Conclusion: CARE通过考虑激活协方差结构和分层秩分配，显著提升了MLA转换的质量，在固定KV宽度下实现了更好的性能，为高效推理提供了有效的注意力模块转换方案。

Abstract: Converting pretrained attention modules such as grouped-query attention (GQA) into multi-head latent attention (MLA) can improve expressivity without increasing KV-cache cost, making it attractive for efficient inference. However, many practical conversion baselines rely on weight-only low-rank approximations (e.g., SVD-style initializations) and uniform rank allocation. They focus on minimizing the difference between weight matrices rather than on how those weights affect input activations, ignore the covariance structure of activations, and enforce uniform rank across layers, causing activation drift and degraded attention fidelity. To address these issues, we propose CARE, a Covariance-Aware, Rank-Enhanced MLA conversion pipeline under a fixed KV width. CARE introduces three key steps: (i) activation-preserving factorization, which aligns the approximation with the actual input activations rather than just the weights; (ii) adjusted-rank allocation, which spreads a fixed KV budget across layers by giving more capacity to layers that need it most; and (iii) KV-parity mapping, which reparameterizes the converted K and V to fit the MLA format while keeping the KV-cache size unchanged. Our method outperforms a uniform-rank SVD baseline on Qwen3-4B/30B-A3B-Instruct-2507 and Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct, reducing one-shot perplexity by up to 215x and improving mean accuracy by up to 1.70x at matched KV budgets. With a brief post-SVD healing fine-tune, we fully recover the original model's accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Embodied Foundation Models at the Edge: A Survey of Deployment Constraints and Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16952)
*Utkarsh Grover,Ravi Ranjan,Mingyang Mao,Trung Tien Dong,Satvik Praveen,Zhenqi Wu,J. Morris Chang,Tinoosh Mohsenin,Yi Sheng,Agoritsa Polyzou,Eiman Kanjo,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将基础模型部署到具身边缘系统本质上是系统问题而非单纯模型压缩问题，需在严格尺寸、重量和功耗约束下实现实时控制，涉及内存流量、计算延迟、时序变异性和安全裕度等多重因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基础模型在具身边缘系统部署面临系统性挑战，需要超越模型压缩的视角，从系统层面解决内存带宽、计算延迟、时序可靠性等实际问题，确保在严格SWaP约束下的可靠运行。

Method: 提出"部署考验"框架，将约束组织为八个相互耦合的障碍；分析代表性边缘工作负载，比较自回归视觉-语言-动作策略与基于扩散的控制器的性能瓶颈；强调系统级协同设计方法。

Result: 自回归VLA策略主要受内存带宽限制，而基于扩散的控制器更多受计算延迟和持续执行成本限制；可靠部署需要跨内存、调度、通信和模型架构的系统级协同设计，包括分离快速控制与慢速语义推理的分解策略。

Conclusion: 具身基础模型的成功部署需要系统级协同设计方法，而非单纯关注模型压缩；"部署考验"框架为评估和解决实际部署障碍提供了结构化方法，强调分解架构的重要性以实现可靠边缘计算。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models in embodied edge systems is fundamentally a systems problem, not just a problem of model compression. Real-time control must operate within strict size, weight, and power constraints, where memory traffic, compute latency, timing variability, and safety margins interact directly. The Deployment Gauntlet organizes these constraints into eight coupled barriers that determine whether embodied foundation models can run reliably in practice. Across representative edge workloads, autoregressive Vision-Language-Action policies are constrained primarily by memory bandwidth, whereas diffusion-based controllers are limited more by compute latency and sustained execution cost. Reliable deployment therefore depends on system-level co-design across memory, scheduling, communication, and model architecture, including decompositions that separate fast control from slower semantic reasoning.

</details>


### [193] [Rewarding DINO: Predicting Dense Rewards with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16978)
*Pierre Krack,Tobias Jülg,Wolfram Burgard,Florian Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rewarding DINO是一种语言条件奖励建模方法，通过从专家演示中学习实际奖励函数而非特定轨迹，解决了密集奖励函数设计困难且通常需要特权状态信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计密集奖励函数在机器人操作中具有挑战性，通常需要仅在仿真中可用的特权状态信息，而现实世界实验无法获得。现有方法基于视觉相似性或序列帧排序预测奖励，但会偏向特定解决方案且在演示未覆盖的状态中未定义奖励。

Method: 提出Rewarding DINO方法，这是一种语言条件奖励建模方法，从专家演示中学习实际奖励函数而非特定轨迹。模型紧凑，可直接替代解析奖励函数，计算开销较低。使用基于排名的损失在24个Meta-World+任务的数据上进行训练。

Result: Rewarding DINO在训练集任务中取得竞争性性能，并在仿真和现实世界的新设置中表现出泛化能力，表明其学习了任务语义。模型与现成的强化学习算法结合，成功解决了Meta-World+训练集中的任务。

Conclusion: Rewarding DINO能够从视觉输入中学习语言条件的奖励函数，在保持模型紧凑的同时实现良好的泛化能力，可作为解析奖励函数的直接替代方案，为机器人操作中的密集奖励设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Well-designed dense reward functions in robot manipulation not only indicate whether a task is completed but also encode progress along the way. Generally, designing dense rewards is challenging and usually requires access to privileged state information available only in simulation, not in real-world experiments. This makes reward prediction models that infer task state information from camera images attractive. A common approach is to predict rewards from expert demonstrations based on visual similarity or sequential frame ordering. However, this biases the resulting reward function towards a specific solution and leaves it undefined in states not covered by the demonstrations. In this work, we introduce Rewarding DINO, a method for language-conditioned reward modeling that learns actual reward functions rather than specific trajectories. The model's compact size allows it to serve as a direct replacement for analytical reward functions with comparatively low computational overhead. We train our model on data sampled from 24 Meta-World+ tasks using a rank-based loss and evaluate pairwise accuracy, rank correlation, and calibration. Rewarding DINO achieves competitive performance in tasks from the training set and generalizes to new settings in simulation and the real world, indicating that it learns task semantics. We also test the model with off-the-shelf reinforcement learning algorithms to solve tasks from our Meta-World+ training set.

</details>


### [194] [Contingency-Aware Planning via Certified Neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17022)
*Kasidit Muenprasitivej,Derya Aksaray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个结合学习型可达性分析与采样规划的多目标导航框架，通过傅里叶神经算子近似HJ方程解，提供理论安全保证，并在未知环境中实现实时规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hamilton-Jacobi可达性分析能提供形式化安全保证，但求解高维偏微分方程限制了其在实时规划中的应用。需要在未知环境中实现具有安全保证的多目标导航。

Method: 使用傅里叶神经算子近似Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs变分不等式的解算子；提供学习可达集的理论下近似保证；将认证可达集与增量多目标规划器集成，强制执行可达集约束和恢复策略。

Result: 框架实现了具有可证明应急行为的渐近最优导航；在KUKA youBot的Webots仿真中进行了实时部署验证。

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功整合了学习型可达性与采样规划，在未知环境中实现了具有形式化安全保证的实时多目标导航，解决了HJ可达性分析的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability provides formal safety guarantees for dynamical systems, but solving high-dimensional HJ partial differential equations limits its use in real-time planning. This paper presents a contingency-aware multi-goal navigation framework that integrates learning-based reachability with sampling-based planning in unknown environments. We use Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) to approximate the solution operator of the Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs variational inequality under varying obstacle configurations. We first provide a theoretical under-approximation guarantee on the safe backward reach-avoid set, which enables formal safety certification of the learned reachable sets. Then, we integrate the certified reachable sets with an incremental multi-goal planner, which enforces reachable-set constraints and a recovery policy that guarantees finite-time return to a safe region. Overall, we demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves asymptotically optimal navigation with provable contingency behavior, and validate its performance through real-time deployment on KUKA's youBot in Webots simulation.

</details>


### [195] [TeleDex: Accessible Dexterous Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17065)
*Omar Rayyan,Maximilian Gillesm,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TeleDex：基于智能手机的开源系统，用于灵巧手和机器人操作器的直观遥操作，无需外部跟踪基础设施，支持快速收集演示数据以进行策略微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据集规模和模型容量不断增加，机器人操作策略仍难以泛化到训练分布之外。在新环境、新任务或新机器人本体中部署最先进策略通常需要收集额外演示数据。需要能够在真实部署环境中快速、经济、最小化设置地收集演示的工具。

Method: 开发了TeleDex系统，使用任何现成手机进行灵巧手和机器人操作器的遥操作。系统从手机流式传输低延迟的6自由度手腕姿态和21自由度手部状态估计，无需外部跟踪基础设施即可重定向到机器人手臂和多指手。支持手持手机模式和可选的3D打印手部安装界面进行手指级遥操作。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中评估了系统，证明了其作为统一可扩展机器人遥操作接口的有效性。通过降低灵巧遥操作的硬件和设置门槛，使用户能够在部署过程中快速收集演示以支持策略微调。

Conclusion: TeleDex提供了一个开源、易用的遥操作解决方案，显著降低了灵巧机器人遥操作的门槛，支持快速演示收集和策略适应，所有软件和硬件设计均已开源。

Abstract: Despite increasing dataset scale and model capacity, robot manipulation policies still struggle to generalize beyond their training distributions. As a result, deploying state-of-the-art policies in new environments, tasks, or robot embodiments often requires collecting additional demonstrations. Enabling this in real-world deployment settings requires tools that allow users to collect demonstrations quickly, affordably, and with minimal setup. We present TeleDex, an open-source system for intuitive teleoperation of dexterous hands and robotic manipulators using any readily available phone. The system streams low-latency 6-DoF wrist poses and articulated 21-DoF hand state estimates from the phone, which are retargeted to robot arms and multi-fingered hands without requiring external tracking infrastructure. TeleDex supports both a handheld phone-only mode and an optional 3D-printable hand-mounted interface for finger-level teleoperation. By lowering the hardware and setup barriers to dexterous teleoperation, TeleDex enables users to quickly collect demonstrations during deployment to support policy fine-tuning. We evaluate the system across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness as a unified scalable interface for robot teleoperation. All software and hardware designs, along with demonstration videos, are open-source and available at orayyan.com/teledex.

</details>


### [196] [SLowRL: Safe Low-Rank Adaptation Reinforcement Learning for Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17092)
*Elham Daneshmand,Shafeef Omar,Glen Berseth,Majid Khadiv,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLowRL框架结合LoRA低秩适应与恢复策略，实现安全高效的机器人运动策略仿真到现实迁移，减少46.5%微调时间并实现近零安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决仿真到现实迁移中运动策略性能下降问题，避免直接在硬件上微调带来的机械故障风险和样本效率低下挑战。

Method: 提出SLowRL框架，结合低秩适应(LoRA)和训练时安全约束，通过恢复策略确保安全，在Unitree Go2四足机器人上评估跳跃和慢跑任务。

Result: 实验显示方法减少46.5%微调时间，实现近零安全违规，仅需秩1适应即可恢复预训练性能，保持稳定安全的现实世界微调。

Conclusion: SLowRL为动态现实世界机器人应用提供了实用、安全、高效的微调方法，证明了低秩适应与安全约束结合的有效性。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer of locomotion policies often leads to performance degradation due to the inevitable sim-to-real gap. Naively fine-tuning these policies directly on hardware is problematic, as it poses risks of mechanical failure and suffers from high sample inefficiency. In this paper, we address the challenge of safely and efficiently fine-tuning reinforcement learning (RL) policies for dynamic locomotion tasks. Specifically, we focus on fine-tuning policies learned in simulation directly on hardware, while explicitly enforcing safety constraints. In doing so, we introduce SLowRL, a framework that combines Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with training-time safety enforcement via a recovery policy. We evaluate our method both in simulation and on a real Unitree Go2 quadruped robot for jump and trot tasks. Experimental results show that our method achieves a $46.5\%$ reduction in fine-tuning time and near-zero safety violations compared to standard proximal policy optimization (PPO) baselines. Notably, we find that a rank-1 adaptation alone is sufficient to recover pre-trained performance in the real world, while maintaining stable and safe real-world fine-tuning. These results demonstrate the practicality of safe, efficient fine-tuning for dynamic real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [197] [Shielded Reinforcement Learning Under Dynamic Temporal Logic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17152)
*Sadık Bera Yüksel,Ali Tevfik Buyukkocak,Derya Aksaray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合顺序控制屏障函数与无模型强化学习的框架，确保在学习过程中满足信号时序逻辑任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人应用中部署受限，需要超越传统安全约束的复杂时空任务规范，如周期性充电或时间限制的区域访问

Method: 利用顺序控制屏障函数和无模型强化学习，确保给定的信号时序逻辑任务在学习过程中得到满足，能够处理具有未知轨迹的动态目标

Result: 通过多种仿真验证了框架的有效性，能够强制执行丰富的STL规范

Conclusion: 该框架扩展了传统安全约束，能够处理涉及动态目标的复杂时空任务，为强化学习在现实系统中的安全部署提供了新方法

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has shown promise in various robotics applications, yet its deployment on real systems is still limited due to safety and operational constraints. The safe RL field has gained considerable attention in recent years, which focuses on imposing safety constraints throughout the learning process. However, real systems often require more complex constraints than just safety, such as periodic recharging or time-bounded visits to specific regions. Imposing such spatio-temporal tasks during learning still remains a challenge. Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is a formal language for specifying temporal properties of real-valued signals and provides a way to express such complex tasks. In this paper, we propose a framework that leverages sequential control barrier functions and model-free RL to ensure that the given STL tasks are satisfied throughout the learning process. Our method extends beyond traditional safety constraints by enforcing rich STL specifications, which can involve visits to dynamic targets with unknown trajectories. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework through various simulations.

</details>


### [198] [SLAM Adversarial Lab: An Extensible Framework for Visual SLAM Robustness Evaluation under Adverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17165)
*Mohamed Hefny,Karthik Dantu,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAL是一个用于评估视觉SLAM系统在对抗性条件下（如雾、雨）性能的模块化框架，支持可解释的严重程度级别和可扩展架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉SLAM系统在现实世界对抗性条件下的鲁棒性评估缺乏系统化框架，需要一种模块化、可扩展的方法来评估不同SLAM算法在各种对抗性条件下的性能。

Method: SAL将对抗性条件表示为扰动，通过可解释的真实世界单位（如雾的能见度米数）支持严重程度级别。采用可扩展架构解耦数据集、扰动和SLAM算法，并包含搜索程序来找到SLAM系统失效的扰动严重程度。

Result: 评估集成了7种SLAM算法，在3个数据集上测试了天气、相机和视频传输扰动，展示了SAL能够系统评估SLAM系统在对抗性条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SAL提供了一个模块化、可扩展的框架，能够系统评估视觉SLAM系统在各种对抗性条件下的性能，有助于理解SLAM系统的鲁棒性边界。

Abstract: We present SAL (SLAM Adversarial Lab), a modular framework for evaluating visual SLAM systems under adversarial conditions such as fog and rain. SAL represents each adversarial condition as a perturbation that transforms an existing dataset into an adversarial dataset. When transforming a dataset, SAL supports severity levels using easily-interpretable real-world units such as meters for fog visibility. SAL's extensible architecture decouples datasets, perturbations, and SLAM algorithms through common interfaces, so users can add new components without rewriting integration code. Moreover, SAL includes a search procedure that finds the severity level of a perturbation at which a SLAM system fails. To showcase the capabilities of SAL, our evaluation integrates seven SLAM algorithms and evaluates them across three datasets under weather, camera, and video transport perturbations.

</details>


### [199] [Influence of Gripper Design on Human Demonstration Quality for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17189)
*Gina L. Georgadarellis,Natalija Beslic,Seonhun Lee,Frank C. Sup,Meghan E. Huber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估手持式夹爪工具UMI在演示医疗包装开启任务中的可用性，发现集中载荷夹爪优于分布式载荷夹爪，但仍远不如徒手操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗包装开启是医疗机器人的重要技能，通过演示学习可以让机器人直接从人类学习操作技能，手持式夹爪工具如UMI提供了高效数据收集途径，但这些工具的有效性高度依赖其可用性

Method: 评估UMI在演示绷带开启任务中的表现，测试三种条件：分布式载荷夹爪、集中载荷夹爪和徒手操作。8名参与者进行计时试验，通过成功率、完成时间和损坏情况评估任务表现，同时使用NASA-TLX问卷评估感知工作量

Result: 集中载荷夹爪相对于分布式载荷夹爪改善了性能，但在速度和效果上仍显著慢于且不如徒手操作。手持式夹爪工具在演示医疗包装开启任务中仍存在可用性限制

Conclusion: 手持式夹爪工具需要进一步的人体工程学和机械改进，以减少用户负担并提高演示质量，特别是在医疗机器人应用中。工具设计对演示学习的效果至关重要

Abstract: Opening sterile medical packaging is routine for healthcare workers but remains challenging for robots. Learning from demonstration enables robots to acquire manipulation skills directly from humans, and handheld gripper tools such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) offer a pathway for efficient data collection. However, the effectiveness of these tools depends heavily on their usability. We evaluated UMI in demonstrating a bandage opening task, a common manipulation task in hospital settings, by testing three conditions: distributed load grippers, concentrated load grippers, and bare hands. Eight participants performed timed trials, with task performance assessed by success rate, completion time, and damage, alongside perceived workload using the NASA-TLX questionnaire. Concentrated load grippers improved performance relative to distributed load grippers but remained substantially slower and less effective than hands. These results underscore the importance of ergonomic and mechanical refinements in handheld grippers to reduce user burden and improve demonstration quality, especially for applications in healthcare robotics.

</details>


### [200] [FastLoop: Parallel Loop Closing with GPU-Acceleration in Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17201)
*Soudabeh Mohammadhashemi,Shishir Gopinath,Kimia Khabiri,Parsa Hosseininejad,Karthik Dantu,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastLoop：基于GPU加速的视觉SLAM闭环模块，通过并行优化解决闭环计算瓶颈，在保持精度的同时显著提升速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉SLAM系统中闭环检测需要在整个地图中搜索匹配，计算复杂度高，成为性能瓶颈。传统CPU实现难以满足实时性要求，特别是在大规模场景中。

Method: 1. 识别视觉SLAM闭环流水线的关键性能瓶颈；2. 采用任务级和数据级并行优化；3. 集成GPU加速的姿态图优化；4. 基于ORB-SLAM3框架，使用CUDA进行GPU编程实现。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上，闭环模块在桌面和嵌入式平台分别获得1.4倍和1.3倍加速；在TUM-VI数据集上分别获得3.0倍和2.4倍加速，同时保持与原始系统相同的精度。

Conclusion: FastLoop通过GPU并行优化有效解决了视觉SLAM闭环检测的计算瓶颈，在多个数据集和平台上实现了显著的性能提升，同时保持了系统精度，为实时大规模SLAM应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visual SLAM systems combine visual tracking with global loop closure to maintain a consistent map and accurate localization. Loop closure is a computationally expensive process as we need to search across the whole map for matches. This paper presents FastLoop, a GPU-accelerated loop closing module to alleviate this computational complexity. We identify key performance bottlenecks in the loop closing pipeline of visual SLAM and address them through parallel optimizations on the GPU. Specifically, we use task-level and data-level parallelism and integrate a GPU-accelerated pose graph optimization. Our implementation is built on top of ORB-SLAM3 and leverages CUDA for GPU programming. Experimental results show that FastLoop achieves an average speedup of 1.4x and 1.3x on the EuRoC dataset and 3.0x and 2.4x on the TUM-VI dataset for the loop closing module on desktop and embedded platforms, respectively, while maintaining the accuracy of the original system.

</details>


### [201] [Visual SLAM with DEM Anchoring for Lunar Surface Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17229)
*Adam Dai,Guillem Casadesus Vila,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于月球长距离导航的立体视觉SLAM系统，结合学习特征检测匹配与数字高程模型全局约束，在极端光照和低纹理环境下减少漂移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来月球任务需要自主漫游车在挑战性地形上长距离导航，但缺乏全球定位系统、极端光照和低纹理月壤导致视觉惯性里程计在长距离导航中积累漂移

Method: 提出立体视觉SLAM系统：前端使用学习特征提取和匹配应对极端光照和重复地形；后端将DEM导出的高度和表面法线因子集成到姿态图中，提供绝对表面约束

Result: 使用Unreal Engine模拟的月球穿越数据和埃特纳山真实月球/火星模拟数据进行验证，DEM锚定相比基线SLAM方法持续减少绝对轨迹误差，在长距离导航中降低漂移

Conclusion: 集成学习特征匹配与DEM全局约束的SLAM系统能有效应对月球长距离导航挑战，在重复或视觉混淆地形中显著减少漂移

Abstract: Future lunar missions will require autonomous rovers capable of traversing tens of kilometers across challenging terrain while maintaining accurate localization and producing globally consistent maps. However, the absence of global positioning systems, extreme illumination, and low-texture regolith make long-range navigation on the Moon particularly difficult, as visual-inertial odometry pipelines accumulate drift over extended traverses. To address this challenge, we present a stereo visual simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system that integrates learned feature detection and matching with global constraints from digital elevation models (DEMs). Our front-end employs learning-based feature extraction and matching to achieve robustness to illumination extremes and repetitive terrain, while the back-end incorporates DEM-derived height and surface-normal factors into a pose graph, providing absolute surface constraints that mitigate long-term drift. We validate our approach using both simulated lunar traverse data generated in Unreal Engine and real Moon/Mars analog data collected from Mt. Etna. Results demonstrate that DEM anchoring consistently reduces absolute trajectory error compared to baseline SLAM methods, lowering drift in long-range navigation even in repetitive or visually aliased terrain.

</details>


### [202] [Full Stack Navigation, Mapping, and Planning for the Lunar Autonomy Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17232)
*Adam Dai,Asta Wu,Keidai Iiyama,Guillem Casadesus Vila,Kaila Coimbra,Thomas Deng,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个用于月球表面导航与建图的模块化全栈自主系统，在GNSS拒止的视觉挑战环境中，集成了语义分割、立体视觉里程计、带闭环的位姿图SLAM以及分层规划与控制，在月球自主挑战赛中取得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在GNSS拒止、视觉环境恶劣的月球表面进行自主导航和建图的挑战，为月球自主挑战赛开发一个鲁棒且高效的自主系统。

Method: 采用模块化架构，整合了基于轻量级学习模型的实时语义分割和特征跟踪，使用立体视觉里程计进行局部定位，通过因子图后端实现带闭环的位姿图SLAM以保持全局一致性定位，采用分层规划方法：高层路径点规划促进建图覆盖和频繁闭环，局部运动规划使用弧线采样结合几何障碍物检查实现高效反应式控制。

Result: 在高保真月球模拟器中评估，展示了厘米级定位精度、高保真地图生成能力，在不同随机种子和岩石分布下具有强重复性，在最终竞赛评估中获得第一名。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一个模块化、全栈的月球表面自主系统，在GNSS拒止的挑战性环境中实现了鲁棒的导航和建图，通过竞赛验证了其有效性和可靠性。

Abstract: We present a modular, full-stack autonomy system for lunar surface navigation and mapping developed for the Lunar Autonomy Challenge. Operating in a GNSS-denied, visually challenging environment, our pipeline integrates semantic segmentation, stereo visual odometry, pose graph SLAM with loop closures, and layered planning and control. We leverage lightweight learning-based perception models for real-time segmentation and feature tracking and use a factor-graph backend to maintain globally consistent localization. High-level waypoint planning is designed to promote mapping coverage while encouraging frequent loop closures, and local motion planning uses arc sampling with geometric obstacle checks for efficient, reactive control. We evaluate our approach in the competition's high-fidelity lunar simulator, demonstrating centimeter-level localization accuracy, high-fidelity map generation, and strong repeatability across random seeds and rock distributions. Our solution achieved first place in the final competition evaluation.

</details>


### [203] [Neural Radiance Maps for Extraterrestrial Navigation and Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17236)
*Adam Dai,Shubh Gupta,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用神经辐射场（NeRF）构建火星车自主导航地图，通过核岭回归整合局部与全局信息，实现高效路径规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前火星车自主导航受限于缺乏易于构建和存储的全局地图，限制了探索效率和科学目标达成。需要一种能够从稀疏2D图像构建详细3D场景表示的方法，以支持在线重规划。

Method: 提出使用NeRF构建导航地图，通过核岭回归在NeRF提取的地形特征上插值局部代价观测，整合局部与全局信息，使火星车能够在在线操作中发现不可通行区域时重新规划路径。

Result: 在高保真仿真中验证了该方法，相比多种基线方法，实现了更低的路径代价和更高的成功率。

Conclusion: NeRF可作为有效的3D场景表示用于自主导航地图构建，提出的规划框架能够整合局部与全局信息，提升火星车在未知环境中的导航效率和安全性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles such as the Mars rovers currently lead the vanguard of surface exploration on extraterrestrial planets and moons. In order to accelerate the pace of exploration and science objectives, it is critical to plan safe and efficient paths for these vehicles. However, current rover autonomy is limited by a lack of global maps which can be easily constructed and stored for onboard re-planning. Recently, Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) have been introduced as a detailed 3D scene representation which can be trained from sparse 2D images and efficiently stored. We propose to use NeRFs to construct maps for online use in autonomous navigation, and present a planning framework which leverages the NeRF map to integrate local and global information. Our approach interpolates local cost observations across global regions using kernel ridge regression over terrain features extracted from the NeRF map, allowing the rover to re-route itself around untraversable areas discovered during online operation. We validate our approach in high-fidelity simulation and demonstrate lower cost and higher percentage success rate path planning compared to various baselines.

</details>


### [204] [DexEXO: A Wearability-First Dexterous Exoskeleton for Operator-Agnostic Demonstration and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17323)
*Alvin Zhu,Mingzhang Zhu,Beom Jun Kim,Jose Victor S. H. Ramos,Yike Shi,Yufeng Wu,Raayan Dhar,Fuyi Yang,Ruochen Hou,Hanzhang Fang,Quanyou Wang,Yuchen Cui,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexEXO：一种以穿戴性优先的手部外骨骼，通过硬件层面的视觉、接触几何和运动学对齐，简化了灵巧机器人学习的演示数据收集和策略训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧机器人学习的规模化受到高质量演示数据收集困难的限制。现有可穿戴接口通常在舒适性和跨用户适应性方面妥协，且演示与部署之间的具身不匹配需要视觉后处理才能进行策略训练。

Method: 提出DexEXO手部外骨骼，采用穿戴性优先设计，在硬件层面实现视觉外观、接触几何和运动学的对齐。包括姿态容忍的拇指机制和基于滑块的手指接口，支持140-217mm手长范围。被动手部视觉上匹配部署的机器人，可直接从原始腕部RGB观测进行策略训练。

Result: 用户研究表明相比现有可穿戴系统具有更好的舒适性和可用性。仅使用视觉对齐的观测训练扩散策略，实现了有竞争力的性能，同时大幅简化了端到端流程。

Conclusion: 优先考虑穿戴性和硬件层面的具身对齐，既减少了人类操作瓶颈，也降低了算法瓶颈，同时不牺牲任务性能，为规模化灵巧机器人学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling dexterous robot learning is constrained by the difficulty of collecting high-quality demonstrations across diverse operators. Existing wearable interfaces often trade comfort and cross-user adaptability for kinematic fidelity, while embodiment mismatch between demonstration and deployment requires visual post-processing before policy training. We present DexEXO, a wearability-first hand exoskeleton that aligns visual appearance, contact geometry, and kinematics at the hardware level. DexEXO features a pose-tolerant thumb mechanism and a slider-based finger interface analytically modeled to support hand lengths from 140~mm to 217~mm, reducing operator-specific fitting and enabling scalable cross-operator data collection. A passive hand visually matches the deployed robot, allowing direct policy training from raw wrist-mounted RGB observations. User studies demonstrate improved comfort and usability compared to prior wearable systems. Using visually aligned observations alone, we train diffusion policies that achieve competitive performance while substantially simplifying the end-to-end pipeline. These results show that prioritizing wearability and hardware-level embodiment alignment reduces both human and algorithmic bottlenecks without sacrificing task performance. Project Page: https://dexexo-research.github.io/

</details>


### [205] [OmniVLN: Omnidirectional 3D Perception and Token-Efficient LLM Reasoning for Visual-Language Navigation across Air and Ground Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17351)
*Zhongyuang Liu,Min He,Shaonan Yu,Xinhang Xu,Muqing Cao,Jianping Li,Jianfei Yang,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniVLN：零样本视觉语言导航框架，结合全向3D感知与分层推理，通过动态场景图和多分辨率空间注意力提示，提升多房间环境中的目标定位精度和导航成功率，同时大幅减少提示令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导的具身导航系统在真实室内环境中存在局限性：窄视场传感导致局部场景感知不完整，需要频繁旋转延迟目标发现；直接使用密集3D地图或详尽物体列表会超出LLM上下文预算。需要一种既能保持精细局部细节又能紧凑表示长距离记忆的解决方案。

Method: OmniVLN融合旋转LiDAR和全景视觉构建硬件无关的建图栈，增量构建五层动态场景图（从网格几何到房间和建筑级结构），通过持久同调房间分割和几何/VLM混合关系验证稳定高层拓扑。导航时将全局DSG转换为以智能体为中心的3D八分体表示，配合多分辨率空间注意力提示，使LLM能渐进过滤候选房间、推断自我中心朝向、定位目标物体并发出可执行导航原语。

Result: 实验表明，分层接口将空间指代精度从77.27%提升至93.18%，在杂乱多房间环境中累计提示令牌减少高达61.7%，导航成功率比平面列表基线提高达11.68%。

Conclusion: OmniVLN通过全向感知与分层推理的有效结合，解决了现有视觉语言导航系统在真实室内环境中的关键限制，显著提升了目标定位精度和导航效率，同时大幅降低了计算开销。该框架为空中和地面机器人的零样本导航提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Language-guided embodied navigation requires an agent to interpret object-referential instructions, search across multiple rooms, localize the referenced target, and execute reliable motion toward it. Existing systems remain limited in real indoor environments because narrow field-of-view sensing exposes only a partial local scene at each step, often forcing repeated rotations, delaying target discovery, and producing fragmented spatial understanding; meanwhile, directly prompting LLMs with dense 3D maps or exhaustive object lists quickly exceeds the context budget. We present OmniVLN, a zero-shot visual-language navigation framework that couples omnidirectional 3D perception with token-efficient hierarchical reasoning for both aerial and ground robots. OmniVLN fuses a rotating LiDAR and panoramic vision into a hardware-agnostic mapping stack, incrementally constructs a five-layer Dynamic Scene Graph (DSG) from mesh geometry to room- and building-level structure, and stabilizes high-level topology through persistent-homology-based room partitioning and hybrid geometric/VLM relation verification. For navigation, the global DSG is transformed into an agent-centric 3D octant representation with multi-resolution spatial attention prompting, enabling the LLM to progressively filter candidate rooms, infer egocentric orientation, localize target objects, and emit executable navigation primitives while preserving fine local detail and compact long-range memory. Experiments show that the proposed hierarchical interface improves spatial referring accuracy from 77.27\% to 93.18\%, reduces cumulative prompt tokens by up to 61.7\% in cluttered multi-room settings, and improves navigation success by up to 11.68\% over a flat-list baseline. We will release the code and an omnidirectional multimodal dataset to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [206] [SafeLand: Safe Autonomous Landing in Unknown Environments with Bayesian Semantic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17430)
*Markus Gross,Andreas Greiner,Sai Bharadhwaj Matha,Felix Soest,Daniel Cremers,Henri Meeß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeLand是一个仅使用摄像头和轻量高度传感器的视觉自主着陆系统，无需先验信息，通过在线语义建图、贝叶斯概率滤波和行为树控制，在未知动态环境中实现安全着陆，响应延迟低于1秒，成功率95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在未知动态环境中的自主着陆存在重大安全挑战，现有方法依赖先验地图、重型传感器或静态标记，无法处理非合作动态障碍物（如人类），限制了泛化能力和实时性能。

Method: 1. 基于深度学习的语义分割构建在线语义地面地图（在7个公开航空数据集上训练，20类mIoU 70.22%）；2. 贝叶斯概率滤波结合时间语义衰减进行地图精化；3. 行为树控制自适应着陆过程，实时响应动态障碍物（暂停、爬升或重选着陆点）。

Result: 在200次仿真和60次端到端实地测试中（工业、城市、乡村环境，高度达100米），实现人类检测零漏报，响应延迟低于1秒，成功率95%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SafeLand提供了一种轻量、无需先验信息的视觉自主着陆解决方案，在动态环境中实现高安全性，为空中机器人研究提供了可扩展的框架和开源实现。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) in unknown, dynamic environments poses significant safety challenges, particularly near people and infrastructure, as UAVs transition to routine urban and rural operations. Existing methods often rely on prior maps, heavy sensors like LiDAR, static markers, or fail to handle non-cooperative dynamic obstacles like humans, limiting generalization and real-time performance. To address these challenges, we introduce SafeLand, a lean, vision-based system for safe autonomous landing (SAL) that requires no prior information and operates only with a camera and a lightweight height sensor. Our approach constructs an online semantic ground map via deep learning-based semantic segmentation, optimized for embedded deployment and trained on a consolidation of seven curated public aerial datasets (achieving 70.22% mIoU across 20 classes), which is further refined through Bayesian probabilistic filtering with temporal semantic decay to robustly identify metric-scale landing spots. A behavior tree then governs adaptive landing, iteratively validates the spot, and reacts in real time to dynamic obstacles by pausing, climbing, or rerouting to alternative spots, maximizing human safety. We extensively evaluate our method in 200 simulations and 60 end-to-end field tests across industrial, urban, and rural environments at altitudes up to 100m, demonstrating zero false negatives for human detection. Compared to the state of the art, SafeLand achieves sub-second response latency, substantially lower than previous methods, while maintaining a superior success rate of 95%. To facilitate further research in aerial robotics, we release SafeLand's segmentation model as a plug-and-play ROS package, available at https://github.com/markus-42/SafeLand.

</details>


### [207] [P$^{3}$Nav: End-to-End Perception, Prediction and Planning for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17459)
*Tianfu Li,Wenbo Chen,Haoxuan Xu,Xinhu Zheng,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P³Nav是一个端到端的视觉语言导航框架，通过统一感知、预测和规划三个模块来增强场景理解，在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法主要关注视觉-文本对齐来构建强大的规划器，但往往忽略了规划前对场景的全面理解需求，导致智能体感知和预测能力不足。

Method: 提出P³Nav框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 感知模块从物体级和地图级提取互补线索；2) 预测模块通过预测航点来建模智能体未来潜在状态；3) 规划模块基于感知和预测线索执行导航任务。

Result: 在REVERIE、R2R-CE和RxR-CE三个基准测试上取得了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过统一感知、预测和规划，P³Nav增强了VLN智能体的场景理解能力，提高了导航成功率，证明了全面场景理解在视觉语言导航中的重要性。

Abstract: In Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN), an agent is required to plan a path to the target specified by the language instruction, using its visual observations. Consequently, prevailing VLN methods primarily focus on building powerful planners through visual-textual alignment. However, these approaches often bypass the imperative of comprehensive scene understanding prior to planning, leaving the agent with insufficient perception or prediction capabilities. Thus, we propose P$^{3}$Nav, a novel end-to-end framework integrating perception, prediction, and planning in a unified pipeline to strengthen the VLN agent's scene understanding and boost navigation success. Specifically, P$^{3}$Nav augments perception by extracting complementary cues from object-level and map-level perspectives. Subsequently, our P$^{3}$Nav predicts waypoints to model the agent's potential future states, endowing the agent with intrinsic awareness of candidate positions during navigation. Conditioned on these future waypoints, P$^{3}$Nav further forecasts semantic map cues, enabling proactive planning and reducing the strict reliance on purely historical context. Integrating these perceptual and predictive cues, a holistic planning module finally carries out the VLN tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our P$^{3}$Nav achieves new state-of-the-art performance on the REVERIE, R2R-CE, and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>


### [208] [From Optimizable to Interactable: Mixed Digital Twin-Empowered Testing of Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17497)
*Jianghong Dong,Chunying Yang,Mengchi Cai,Chaoyi Chen,Qing Xu,Jianqiang Wang,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IMPACT框架，通过引入L5级"可交互"数字孪生和混合数字孪生技术，将人类行为不确定性纳入车路协同系统测试，生成高质量极端场景并实现安全测试


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极端场景生成方法主要依赖AI驱动，且车路协同系统测试通常局限于仿真环境，缺乏对人类行为不确定性的考虑和真实环境下的安全测试能力

Method: 引入VICS数字孪生分类中的L5"可交互"级别，提出IMPACT框架，通过混合数字孪生技术实现"物理-虚拟动作交互"，允许人类直接与VICS实体交互，将高度不确定的人类行为纳入测试循环

Result: 在I-VIT测试平台上实现了IMPACT框架，实验证明其有效性，能够生成高质量极端场景补充AI方法，并实现真实环境下的安全测试

Conclusion: IMPACT框架通过引入人类交互和混合数字孪生技术，解决了现有车路协同系统测试方法的局限性，为极端场景下的安全测试提供了新范式

Abstract: Sufficient testing under corner cases is critical for the long-term operation of vehicle-infrastructure cooperation systems (VICS). However, existing corner-case generation methods are primarily AI-driven, and VICS testing under corner cases is typically limited to simulation. In this paper, we introduce an L5 ''Interactable'' level to the VICS digital twin (VICS-DT) taxonomy, extending beyond the conventional L4 ''Optimizable'' level. We further propose an L5-level VICS testing framework, IMPACT (Interactive Mixed-digital-twin Paradigm for Advanced Cooperative vehicle-infrastructure Testing). By enabling direct human interactions with VICS entities, IMPACT incorporates highly uncertain and unpredictable human behaviors into the testing loop, naturally generating high-quality corner cases that complement AI-based methods. Furthermore, the mixedDT-enabled ''Physical-Virtual Action Interaction'' facilitates safe VICS testing under corner cases, incorporating real-world environments and entities rather than purely in simulation. Finally, we implement IMPACT on the I-VIT (Interactive Vehicle-Infrastructure Testbed), and experiments demonstrate its effectiveness. The experimental videos are available at our project website: https://dongjh20.github.io/IMPACT.

</details>


### [209] [Interpreting Context-Aware Human Preferences for Multi-Objective Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17510)
*Tharun Sethuraman,Subham Agrawal,Nils Dengler,Jorge de Heuvel,Teena Hassan,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合基础模型与多目标强化学习的机器人导航框架，使机器人能够理解和应用上下文相关的人类偏好，实现语义推理与运动控制的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类共享环境中工作的机器人不仅需要实现安全高效等任务级导航目标，还需要适应人类偏好。然而，人类偏好通常以自然语言表达且依赖于环境上下文，难以直接集成到低级机器人控制策略中。

Method: 提出一个结合基础模型与多目标强化学习（MORL）导航策略的管道。视觉语言模型（VLM）从机载视觉观察中提取结构化环境上下文，大型语言模型（LLM）将自然语言用户反馈转换为可解释的上下文相关行为规则并存储在持久可更新的规则内存中。偏好翻译模块将上下文信息和存储规则映射为数值化偏好向量，参数化预训练的MORL策略以实现实时导航适应。

Result: 通过定量组件级评估、用户研究和真实世界机器人部署进行评估。结果显示系统能够可靠捕捉用户意图，生成一致的偏好向量，并在不同上下文中实现可控的行为适应。

Conclusion: 所提出的管道提高了机器人在人类共享环境中的适应性、透明度和可用性，同时保持安全、响应迅速的实时控制。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-shared environments must not only achieve task-level navigation objectives such as safety and efficiency, but also adapt their behavior to human preferences. However, as human preferences are typically expressed in natural language and depend on environmental context, it is difficult to directly integrate them into low-level robot control policies. In this work, we present a pipeline that enables robots to understand and apply context-dependent navigation preferences by combining foundational models with a Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) navigation policy. Thus, our approach integrates high-level semantic reasoning with low-level motion control. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) extracts structured environmental context from onboard visual observations, while Large Language Models (LLM) convert natural language user feedback into interpretable, context-dependent behavioral rules stored in a persistent but updatable rule memory. A preference translation module then maps contextual information and stored rules into numerical preference vectors that parameterize a pretrained MORL policy for real-time navigation adaptation. We evaluate the proposed framework through quantitative component-level evaluations, a user study, and real-world robot deployments in various indoor environments. Our results demonstrate that the system reliably captures user intent, generates consistent preference vectors, and enables controllable behavior adaptation across diverse contexts. Overall, the proposed pipeline improves the adaptability, transparency, and usability of robots operating in shared human environments, while maintaining safe and responsive real-time control.

</details>


### [210] [KineVLA: Towards Kinematics-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models with Bi-Level Action Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17524)
*Gaoge Han,Zhengqing Gao,Ziwen Li,Jiaxin Huang,Shaoli Huang,Fakhri Karray,Mingming Gong,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KineVLA框架，解决具有丰富运动学属性的视觉-语言-动作任务，通过双层表示分离目标不变性与运动学可变性，实现精细化和个性化操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作指令仅粗略或部分捕捉运动学属性，无法支持细粒度和个性化操作。需要一种能密集编码方向、轨迹、朝向、相对位移等运动学属性的语言指令，使任务目标保持不变而执行轨迹能适应指令级运动学规范

Method: 提出KineVLA框架：1）显式解耦目标级不变性与运动学级可变性；2）采用双层动作表示；3）使用双层推理令牌作为显式监督的中间变量来对齐语言和动作；4）构建包含仿真和真实机器人平台的运动学感知VLA数据集，具有指令级运动学变化和双层标注

Result: 在LIBERO和Realman-75机器人上的大量实验表明，KineVLA在运动学敏感基准测试中持续优于强VLA基线，实现了更精确、可控和可泛化的操作行为

Conclusion: KineVLA通过显式解耦目标不变性与运动学可变性，有效解决了具有丰富运动学属性的VLA任务，为细粒度和个性化机器人操作提供了新框架

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel kinematics-rich vision-language-action (VLA) task, in which language commands densely encode diverse kinematic attributes (such as direction, trajectory, orientation, and relative displacement) from initiation through completion, at key moments, unlike existing action instructions that capture kinematics only coarsely or partially, thereby supporting fine-grained and personalized manipulation. In this setting, where task goals remain invariant while execution trajectories must adapt to instruction-level kinematic specifications. To address this challenge, we propose KineVLA, a vision-language-action framework that explicitly decouples goal-level invariance from kinematics-level variability through a bi-level action representation and bi-level reasoning tokens to serve as explicit, supervised intermediate variables that align language and action. To support this task, we construct the kinematics-aware VLA datasets spanning both simulation and real-world robotic platforms, featuring instruction-level kinematic variations and bi-level annotations. Extensive experiments on LIBERO and a Realman-75 robot demonstrate that KineVLA consistently outperforms strong VLA baselines on kinematics-sensitive benchmarks, achieving more precise, controllable, and generalizable manipulation behaviors.

</details>


### [211] [HeiSD: Hybrid Speculative Decoding for Embodied Vision-Language-Action Models with Kinematic Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17573)
*Zihao Zheng,Zhihao Mao,Sicheng Tian,Maoliang Li,Jiayu Chen,Xinhao Sun,Zhaobo Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Donggang Cao,Hong Mei,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HeiSD框架，通过混合使用草稿器基和检索基推测解码来加速VLA模型，在保持高任务成功率的同时实现2.45倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型推理速度慢，推测解码是有效加速方法但存在两种类型（草稿器基和检索基）。现有方法未能分析这两种类型在VLA模型中的优劣，导致单独使用或优化，而机器人控制轨迹模式分析表明应该混合使用这两种推测解码

Method: 提出HeiSD框架：1) 检索基推测解码优化方法，包含验证跳过机制和序列级松弛接受策略；2) 基于运动学的融合度量来自动确定混合边界，实现草稿器基和检索基推测解码的智能切换

Result: 实验结果显示，HeiSD在仿真基准测试中达到最高2.45倍加速，在真实场景中达到2.06~2.41倍加速，同时保持高任务成功率

Conclusion: 通过分析VLA模型控制的机器人轨迹模式，发现混合使用两种推测解码的潜力。HeiSD框架有效解决了检索基推测解码的草稿拒绝和持续错误问题，以及混合边界确定难题，实现了显著的推理加速

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Models have become the mainstream solution for robot control, but suffer from slow inference speeds. Speculative Decoding (SD) is a promising acceleration method which can be divided into two categories: drafter-based SD and retrieval-based SD. Existing methods fail to analyze the advantages and disadvantages of these two types of SD in VLA models, leading to their sole application or optimization. In this paper, we analyze the trajectory patterns of robots controlled by the VLA model and derive a key insight: the two types of SD should be used in a hybrid manner. However, achieving hybrid SD in VLA models poses several challenges: (1) draft rejection and persistent errors in retrieval-based SD; (2) difficulty in determining the hybrid boundary. To address these, we propose the HeiSD framework. We propose a retrieval-based SD optimization method in HeiSD,which contains a verify-skip mechanism and a sequence-wise relaxed acceptance strategy. Moreover, we proposed a kinematic-based fused metric in HeiSD to automatically determine the hybrid boundary. Experimental results demonstrate that HeiSD attains a speedup of up to 2.45x in simulation benchmarks and 2.06x~2.41x in real-world scenarios, while sustaining a high task success rate.

</details>


### [212] [VectorWorld: Efficient Streaming World Model via Diffusion Flow on Vector Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17652)
*Chaokang Jiang,Desen Zhou,Jiuming Liu,Kevin Li Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VectorWorld：一种用于自动驾驶闭环评估的流式世界模型，通过增量生成以自我为中心的向量图瓦片，解决了现有生成模型在初始化、实时性和长期稳定性方面的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成世界模型在闭环评估中存在三个主要问题：(1) 无历史初始化导致策略输入不匹配；(2) 多步采样延迟违反实时预算；(3) 长期视野下运动学不可行性累积。需要一种能够支持实时、稳定、长距离闭环仿真的世界模型。

Method: 提出VectorWorld流式世界模型：1) 通过运动感知门控VAE生成策略兼容的交互状态，实现历史条件初始化；2) 使用边缘门控关系DiT进行无求解器一步掩码补全，实现实时外推；3) 引入ΔSim物理对齐非自我策略，采用混合离散-连续动作和可微分运动学对数整形，稳定长期推演。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动和nuPlan数据集上，VectorWorld提高了地图结构保真度和初始化有效性，支持稳定、实时的1公里以上闭环推演。

Conclusion: VectorWorld通过流式向量图生成、历史条件初始化和物理对齐NPC策略，有效解决了自动驾驶闭环评估中世界模型的关键挑战，为交互式仿真提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of autonomous-driving policies requires interactive simulation beyond log replay. However, existing generative world models often degrade in closed loop due to (i) history-free initialization that mismatches policy inputs, (ii) multi-step sampling latency that violates real-time budgets, and (iii) compounding kinematic infeasibility over long horizons. We propose VectorWorld, a streaming world model that incrementally generates ego-centric $64 \mathrm{m}\times 64\mathrm{m}$ lane--agent vector-graph tiles during rollout. VectorWorld aligns initialization with history-conditioned policies by producing a policy-compatible interaction state via a motion-aware gated VAE. It enables real-time outpainting via solver-free one-step masked completion with an edge-gated relational DiT trained with interval-conditioned MeanFlow and JVP-based large-step supervision. To stabilize long-horizon rollouts, we introduce $Δ$Sim, a physics-aligned non-ego (NPC) policy with hybrid discrete--continuous actions and differentiable kinematic logit shaping. On Waymo open motion and nuPlan, VectorWorld improves map-structure fidelity and initialization validity, and supports stable, real-time $1\mathrm{km}+$ closed-loop rollouts (\href{https://github.com/jiangchaokang/VectorWorld}{code}).

</details>


### [213] [Consistency-Driven Dual LSTM Models for Kinematic Control of a Wearable Soft Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17672)
*Xingyu Chen,Yi Xiong,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种一致性驱动的双LSTM框架，用于精确学习可穿戴软体机器人手臂的正逆运动学，解决气动执行器非线性迟滞特性和一对多映射问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动执行器存在非线性迟滞行为，且执行器输入与末端执行器位置之间存在一对多映射关系，传统方法难以准确建模。需要开发能够同时学习正逆运动学并保持物理一致性的方法，以支持可穿戴人机协作系统。

Method: 采用一致性驱动的双LSTM框架，通过循环一致性损失增强物理真实性和逆预测稳定性。框架同时学习正向（执行器输入到末端位置）和逆向（末端位置到执行器输入）运动学模型。

Result: 实验包括轨迹跟踪、消融研究和可穿戴演示，结果表明一致性损失显著提高了预测精度并增强了物理一致性。可穿戴软体机器人手臂在物体交接、障碍物感知抓放、抽屉操作等日常任务中表现出良好的人机协作能力和适应性。

Conclusion: 基于学习的运动学模型在以人为本的可穿戴机器人系统中具有广阔应用前景，所提方法通过一致性损失有效解决了软体气动执行器的建模挑战，为可穿戴人机协作系统提供了可靠的技术基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a consistency-driven dual LSTM framework for accurately learning both the forward and inverse kinematics of a pneumatically actuated soft robotic arm integrated into a wearable device. This approach effectively captures the nonlinear and hysteretic behaviors of soft pneumatic actuators while addressing the one-to-many mapping challenge between actuation inputs and end-effector positions. By incorporating a cycle consistency loss, we enhance physical realism and improve the stability of inverse predictions. Extensive experiments-including trajectory tracking, ablation studies, and wearable demonstrations-confirm the effectiveness of our method. Results indicate that the inclusion of the consistency loss significantly boosts prediction accuracy and promotes physical consistency over conventional approaches. Moreover, the wearable soft robotic arm demonstrates strong human-robot collaboration capabilities and adaptability in everyday tasks such as object handover, obstacle-aware pick-and-place, and drawer operation. This work underscores the promising potential of learning-based kinematic models for human-centric, wearable robotic systems.

</details>


### [214] [AERR-Nav: Adaptive Exploration-Recovery-Reminiscing Strategy for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17712)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Haoyang Yang,Haoang Li,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AERR-Nav：一种零样本物体导航框架，通过自适应探索-恢复-回忆策略和快慢思维模式，在未知多楼层环境中动态调整机器人状态，平衡探索与利用，实现更好的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本物体导航方法在未知多楼层环境中面临挑战，特别是在平衡探索与利用方面存在不足，导致机器人在狭窄交叉口卡住、无限徘徊或无法找到楼梯入口等问题。

Method: 提出AERR-Nav框架，包含两个关键创新：1）自适应探索-恢复-回忆策略，使机器人在三种状态间动态转换以应对不同导航场景；2）自适应探索状态包含快慢思维模式，帮助机器人基于环境信息变化平衡探索、利用和高级推理。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D基准测试上的广泛实验表明，AERR-Nav在零样本方法中达到了最先进的性能。全面的消融研究进一步验证了所提策略和模块的有效性。

Conclusion: AERR-Nav通过动态状态调整机制有效解决了零样本物体导航在未知多楼层环境中的探索-利用平衡问题，显著提升了导航性能。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object Navigation (ZSON) in unknown multi-floor environments presents a significant challenge. Recent methods, mostly based on semantic value greedy waypoint selection, spatial topology-enhanced memory, and Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a decision-making framework, have led to improvements. However, these architectures struggle to balance exploration and exploitation for ZSON when encountering unseen environments, especially in multi-floor settings, such as robots getting stuck at narrow intersections, endlessly wandering, or failing to find stair entrances. To overcome these challenges, we propose AERR-Nav, a Zero-Shot Object Navigation framework that dynamically adjusts its state based on the robot's environment. Specifically, AERR-Nav has the following two key advantages: (1) An Adaptive Exploration-Recovery-Reminiscing Strategy, enables robots to dynamically transition between three states, facilitating specialized responses to diverse navigation scenarios. (2) An Adaptive Exploration State featuring Fast and Slow-Thinking modes helps robots better balance exploration, exploitation, and higher-level reasoning based on evolving environmental information. Extensive experiments on the HM3D and MP3D benchmarks demonstrate that our AERR-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance among zero-shot methods. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the efficacy of our proposed strategy and modules.

</details>


### [215] [VolumeDP: Modeling Volumetric Representation for Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17720)
*Tianxing Zhou,Feiyang Xue,Zhangchen Ye,Tianyuan Yuan,Hang Zhao,Tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VolumeDP：通过3D体积表示解决视觉模仿学习中2D-3D不匹配问题的策略架构，在多个基准测试中实现最先进性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模仿学习方法直接将2D图像观测映射到3D动作输出，造成2D-3D不匹配，阻碍空间推理并降低鲁棒性

Method: 首先通过交叉注意力将图像特征提升为体积表示，然后使用可学习模块选择任务相关体素并转换为紧凑的空间标记集，最后通过多标记解码器基于完整标记集预测动作

Result: 在LIBERO仿真基准测试中达到88.8%的平均成功率，比最强基线提升14.8%；在ManiSkill和LIBERO-Plus基准测试中也大幅优于先前方法；真实世界实验显示更高的成功率和对新空间布局、相机视角和环境背景的鲁棒泛化能力

Conclusion: VolumeDP通过恢复空间对齐的显式3D推理，解决了视觉模仿学习中的2D-3D不匹配问题，实现了最先进的性能并展现出强大的泛化能力

Abstract: Imitation learning is a prominent paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing visual imitation methods map 2D image observations directly to 3D action outputs, imposing a 2D-3D mismatch that hinders spatial reasoning and degrades robustness. We present VolumeDP, a policy architecture that restores spatial alignment by explicitly reasoning in 3D. VolumeDP first lifts image features into a Volumetric Representation via cross-attention. It then selects task-relevant voxels with a learnable module and converts them into a compact set of spatial tokens, markedly reducing computation while preserving action-critical geometry. Finally, a multi-token decoder conditions on the entire token set to predict actions, thereby avoiding lossy aggregation that collapses multiple spatial tokens into a single descriptor. VolumeDP achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate of 88.8% on the LIBERO simulation benchmark, outperforming the strongest baseline by a substantial 14.8% improvement. It also delivers large performance gains over prior methods on the ManiSkill and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. Real-world experiments further demonstrate higher success rates and robust generalization to novel spatial layouts, camera viewpoints, and environment backgrounds. Code will be released.

</details>


### [216] [Multi-Source Human-in-the-Loop Digital Twin Testbed for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles in Mixed Traffic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17751)
*Jianghong Dong,Jiawei Wang,Chunying Yang,Mengchi Cai,Chaoyi Chen,Qing Xu,Jianqiang Wang,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MSH-MCCT是一个创新的CAV测试平台，通过混合数字孪生技术整合物理、虚拟和混合平台，支持真实人类驾驶员与CAV算法在多视场中交互，显著提升混合交通环境下的实验灵活性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合交通环境中，CAV需要与人类驾驶车辆交互，但现有测试平台难以捕捉复杂的CAV-HDV交互，缺乏支持多源真实人类驾驶员参与的灵活测试环境。

Method: 提出MSH-MCCT测试平台，采用混合数字孪生概念（混合现实+数字孪生），整合物理平台、虚拟平台和混合平台，通过混合平台桥接，允许人类驾驶员和CAV算法在多个视场中操作物理和虚拟车辆。

Result: 实验展示了MSH-MCCT在混合交通车辆编队测试中的潜力，能够通过不同保真度的驾驶模拟器实现多源真实人类驾驶员在环的CAV测试，验证了平台的交互能力和灵活性。

Conclusion: MSH-MCCT通过创新的混合数字孪生架构，为CAV测试提供了高度灵活和可扩展的平台，能够有效支持复杂CAV-HDV交互研究，是混合交通环境测试的重要工具。

Abstract: In the emerging mixed traffic environments, Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) have to interact with surrounding human-driven vehicles (HDVs). This paper introduces MSH-MCCT (Multi-Source Human-in-the-Loop Mixed Cloud Control Testbed), a novel CAV testbed that captures complex interactions between various CAVs and HDVs. Utilizing the Mixed Digital Twin concept, which combines Mixed Reality with Digital Twin, MSH-MCCT integrates physical, virtual, and mixed platforms, along with multi-source control inputs. Bridged by the mixed platform, MSH-MCCT allows human drivers and CAV algorithms to operate both physical and virtual vehicles within multiple fields of view. Particularly, this testbed facilitates the coexistence and real-time interaction of physical and virtual CAVs \& HDVs, significantly enhancing the experimental flexibility and scalability. Experiments on vehicle platooning in mixed traffic showcase the potential of MSH-MCCT to conduct CAV testing with multi-source real human drivers in the loop through driving simulators of diverse fidelity. The videos for the experiments are available at our project website: https://dongjh20.github.io/MSH-MCCT.

</details>


### [217] [Huddle: Parallel Shape Assembly using Decentralized, Minimalistic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17768)
*Khai Yi Chin,Tingwei Meng,Zhe Chen,Daniel Bassett,Yuri Ivanov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于去中心化机器人形成任意形状组装的算法，通过局部交互确保全局无不可达状态或间隙，利用信号和对齐机制吸引路过机器人加入组装


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人组装方法通常需要集中控制、精确定位或复杂协调，难以实现大规模、去中心化的任意形状组装。需要一种简单、局部交互的方法来确保全局组装质量

Method: 基于局部交互的算法，仅需附着相邻机器人之间的通信。通过信号和对齐机制，已组装机器人吸引路过机器人加入扩展组装。算法是运动无关的，无需姿态定位，支持异步和顺序无关的组装

Result: 证明了算法的正确性，并在107个机器人的组装实验中验证了有效性，成功形成任意形状的组装体

Conclusion: 该算法提供了一种最小化、去中心化的机器人组装方法，仅需局部邻居通信即可实现任意形状的组装，无需集中控制或精确定位，具有实际应用潜力

Abstract: We propose a novel algorithm for forming arbitrarily shaped assemblies using decentralized robots. By relying on local interactions, the algorithm ensures there are no unreachable states or gaps in the assembly, which are global properties. The in-assembly robots attract passing-by robots into expanding the assembly via a simple implementation of signaling and alignment. Our approach is minimalistic, requiring only communication between attached, immediate neighbors. It is motion-agnostic and requires no pose localization, enabling asynchronous and order-independent assembly. We prove the algorithm's correctness and demonstrate its effectiveness in forming a 107-robot assembly.

</details>


### [218] [EVA: Aligning Video World Models with Executable Robot Actions via Inverse Dynamics Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17808)
*Ruixiang Wang,Qingming Liu,Yueci Deng,Guiliang Liu,Zhen Liu,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EVA是一个强化学习后训练框架，通过可执行性对齐来改进视频世界模型，使其生成的视觉推演更符合机器人物理约束，从而提高下游任务执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频世界模型缺乏显式的可执行性约束，导致视觉上连贯的推演可能违反刚体运动学和动力学一致性，产生不稳定或不可行的控制命令，这种视觉生成与物理可执行控制之间的不匹配被称为"可执行性差距"。

Method: 提出可执行视频对齐（EVA）框架：1）在真实机器人轨迹上训练逆动力学模型（IDM）；2）将IDM重新用作奖励模型，通过生成的视频诱导的动作序列来评估视频质量；3）奖励平滑运动（速度、加速度、急动度），惩罚违反具体化约束的动作；4）即使生成的视频包含严重视觉伪影，奖励仍然有效，因为这些伪影通常转化为不稳定或越界的动作。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准测试和真实双臂机器人上的实验表明，EVA减少了生成推演中特定于具体化的伪影，并提高了下游任务执行的成功率。

Conclusion: EVA通过利用可执行性差距作为训练信号，成功对齐了视频世界模型，使其生成的视觉推演更符合机器人物理约束，从而提高了基于视频世界模型的机器人控制系统的实际性能。

Abstract: Video generative models are increasingly used as world models for robotics, where a model generates a future visual rollout conditioned on the current observation and task instruction, and an inverse dynamics model (IDM) converts the generated frames into executable robot actions. However, current video world models lack explicit executability constraints. As a result, visually coherent rollouts may still violate rigid-body and kinematic consistency, producing unstable or infeasible control commands when decoded by an IDM. We refer to this mismatch between visual generation and physically executable control as the executability gap. While this gap can be mitigated at inference time using techniques such as rejection sampling, such approaches are inefficient due to the high cost of video generation. In this paper, we leverage the executability gap as a training signal and introduce Executable Video Alignment (EVA), a reinforcement-learning post-training framework for aligning video world models. EVA trains an inverse dynamics model on real robot trajectories and repurposes it as a reward model that evaluates generated videos through the action sequences they induce, encouraging smooth motions measured by velocity, acceleration, and jerk while penalizing actions that violate embodiment constraints. Importantly, the reward remains informative even when generated videos contain severe visual artifacts, since such artifacts typically translate into unstable or out-of-bound actions. Experiments on the RoboTwin benchmark and a real bimanual robot show that EVA reduces embodiment-specific artifacts in generated rollouts and improves downstream task execution success.

</details>


### [219] [Generative Control as Optimization: Time Unconditional Flow Matching for Adaptive and Robust Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17834)
*Zunzhe Zhang,Runhan Huang,Yicheng Liu,Shaoting Zhu,Linzhan Mou,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeCO将动作合成从轨迹积分转化为迭代优化，通过学习动作序列空间中的平稳速度场，实现基于收敛情况的自适应计算分配，并提供训练免费的安全信号用于异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流匹配方法在机器人模仿学习中存在结构效率问题：推理过程通常受限于固定的积分调度，无论状态复杂度如何都使用相同的计算预算，导致对简单动作和复杂任务都消耗相同计算资源。

Method: GeCO（Generative Control as Optimization）是一个时间无条件框架，学习动作序列空间中的平稳速度场，使专家行为形成稳定吸引子。测试时推理转化为自适应优化过程，根据收敛情况分配计算：简单状态提前退出，困难状态延长优化。

Result: 在标准仿真基准测试中验证了GeCO的有效性，并成功扩展到pi0系列视觉-语言-动作模型。作为标准流匹配头的即插即用替代方案，GeCO提高了成功率和效率，同时通过优化原生机制实现安全部署。

Conclusion: GeCO通过将动作合成重新定义为优化问题，解决了现有方法的计算效率瓶颈，实现了自适应推理并提供了内置的安全检测能力，为机器人模仿学习提供了更高效、更安全的框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models and flow matching have become a cornerstone of robotic imitation learning, yet they suffer from a structural inefficiency where inference is often bound to a fixed integration schedule that is agnostic to state complexity. This paradigm forces the policy to expend the same computational budget on trivial motions as it does on complex tasks. We introduce Generative Control as Optimization (GeCO), a time-unconditional framework that transforms action synthesis from trajectory integration into iterative optimization. GeCO learns a stationary velocity field in the action-sequence space where expert behaviors form stable attractors. Consequently, test-time inference becomes an adaptive process that allocates computation based on convergence--exiting early for simple states while refining longer for difficult ones. Furthermore, this stationary geometry yields an intrinsic, training-free safety signal, as the field norm at the optimized action serves as a robust out-of-distribution (OOD) detector, remaining low for in-distribution states while significantly increasing for anomalies. We validate GeCO on standard simulation benchmarks and demonstrate seamless scaling to pi0-series Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. As a plug-and-play replacement for standard flow-matching heads, GeCO improves success rates and efficiency with an optimization-native mechanism for safe deployment. Video and code can be found at https://hrh6666.github.io/GeCO/

</details>


### [220] [DexViTac: Collecting Human Visuo-Tactile-Kinematic Demonstrations for Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17851)
*Xitong Chen,Yifeng Pan,Min Li,Xiaotian Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexViTac是一个便携式、以人为中心的数据收集系统，用于接触丰富的灵巧操作，能够高效采集第一人称视觉、高密度触觉、末端执行器位姿和手部运动学数据，并构建了包含2400多个演示的多模态数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模高质量多模态演示对机器人学习接触丰富的灵巧操作至关重要，但现有以人为中心的数据收集系统难以捕捉物理交互中的触觉信息。

Method: 开发了DexViTac便携式数据收集系统，包含第一人称视觉、高密度触觉传感、末端执行器位姿和手部运动学采集功能；提出基于运动学的触觉表示学习算法来解决触觉信号的语义模糊性；构建了包含2400多个视觉-触觉-运动学演示的多模态数据集。

Result: DexViTac实现每小时超过248个演示的收集效率，对复杂视觉遮挡具有鲁棒性；使用该数据集和算法训练的策略在四个挑战性任务中平均成功率超过85%，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: DexViTac系统及其多模态数据集和表示学习算法显著提升了接触丰富灵巧操作的学习性能，验证了该系统对机器人学习的实质性改进。

Abstract: Large-scale, high-quality multimodal demonstrations are essential for robot learning of contact-rich dexterous manipulation. While human-centric data collection systems lower the barrier to scaling, they struggle to capture the tactile information during physical interactions. Motivated by this, we present DexViTac, a portable, human-centric data collection system tailored for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. The system enables the high-fidelity acquisition of first-person vision, high-density tactile sensing, end-effector poses, and hand kinematics within unstructured, in-the-wild environments. Building upon this hardware, we propose a kinematics-grounded tactile representation learning algorithm that effectively resolves semantic ambiguities within tactile signals. Leveraging the efficiency of DexViTac, we construct a multimodal dataset comprising over 2,400 visuo-tactile-kinematic demonstrations. Experiments demonstrate that DexViTac achieves a collection efficiency exceeding 248 demonstrations per hour and remains robust against complex visual occlusions. Real-world deployment confirms that policies trained with the proposed dataset and learning strategy achieve an average success rate exceeding 85% across four challenging tasks. This performance significantly outperforms baseline methods, thereby validating the substantial improvement the system provides for learning contact-rich dexterous manipulation. Project page: https://xitong-c.github.io/DexViTac/.

</details>


### [221] [RoboForge: Physically Optimized Text-guided Whole-Body Locomotion for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17927)
*Xichen Yuan,Zhe Li,Bofan Lyu,Kuangji Zuo,Yanshuo Lu,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一潜在驱动框架，通过双向物理优化将文本生成运动直接转换为类人机器人可行走的全身运动，避免传统重定向方法的物理不可行问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到机器人运动转换流程存在重定向问题：运动学质量受物理不可行性、接触转换错误和真实世界动力学数据成本高的限制，需要更有效的物理可行运动生成方法

Method: 提出双向耦合框架，包含物理可行性优化模块：前向通过教师-学生蒸馏策略和物理可行性奖励优化控制器；后向将奖励优化的模拟数据转换为高质量显式运动数据，用于微调运动生成器，形成自我改进循环

Result: 在Unitree G1类人机器人上实验表明，双向优化提高了跟踪精度和成功率；在IsaacLab和MuJoCo中，潜在驱动流程在精度和稳定性上均优于传统显式重定向基线

Conclusion: 通过将基于扩散的运动生成与物理可行性优化耦合，该框架为可部署的文本引导类人智能提供了实用路径，实现了物理约束下的自然语言到全身运动转换

Abstract: While generative models have become effective at producing human-like motions from text, transferring these motions to humanoid robots for physical execution remains challenging. Existing pipelines are often limited by retargeting, where kinematic quality is undermined by physical infeasibility, contact-transition errors, and the high cost of real-world dynamical data. We present a unified latent-driven framework that bridges natural language and whole-body humanoid locomotion through a retarget-free, physics-optimized pipeline. Rather than treating generation and control as separate stages, our key insight is to couple them bidirectionally under physical constraints.We introduce a Physical Plausibility Optimization (PP-Opt) module as the coupling interface. In the forward direction, PP-Opt refines a teacher-student distillation policy with a plausibility-centric reward to suppress artifacts such as floating, skating, and penetration. In the backward direction, it converts reward-optimized simulation rollouts into high-quality explicit motion data, which is used to fine-tune the motion generator toward a more physically plausible latent distribution. This bidirectional design forms a self-improving cycle: the generator learns a physically grounded latent space, while the controller learns to execute latent-conditioned behaviors with dynamical integrity.Extensive experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid show that our bidirectional optimization improves tracking accuracy and success rates. Across IsaacLab and MuJoCo, the implicit latent-driven pipeline consistently outperforms conventional explicit retargeting baselines in both precision and stability. By coupling diffusion-based motion generation with physical plausibility optimization, our framework provides a practical path toward deployable text-guided humanoid intelligence.

</details>


### [222] [Specification-Aware Distribution Shaping for Robotics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17969)
*Sadık Bera Yüksel,Derya Aksaray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个规范感知的动作分布优化框架，在预训练机器人基础模型执行期间强制执行信号时序逻辑约束，无需修改模型参数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人基础模型虽然能够执行自然语言指令完成多样化任务，但缺乏对安全性和时间相关规范的形式化保证。实际部署中机器人需要遵守包含丰富时空要求的操作约束，如时间限制的目标访问、顺序目标和持续安全条件。

Method: 提出规范感知的动作分布优化框架，在预训练机器人基础模型执行期间强制执行信号时序逻辑约束。在每个决策步骤中，通过前向动力学传播对剩余时间范围进行推理，计算满足硬STL可行性约束的最小修改动作分布，而不修改模型参数。

Result: 在仿真中使用最先进的机器人基础模型，在多个环境和复杂规范下验证了所提出的框架。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在保持机器人基础模型能力的同时，为其提供形式化的时空规范保证，解决了现有数据驱动方法缺乏安全性和规范满足保证的问题。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models have demonstrated strong capabilities in executing natural language instructions across diverse tasks and environments. However, they remain largely data-driven and lack formal guarantees on safety and satisfaction of time-dependent specifications during deployment. In practice, robots often need to comply with operational constraints involving rich spatio-temporal requirements such as time-bounded goal visits, sequential objectives, and persistent safety conditions. In this work, we propose a specification-aware action distribution optimization framework that enforces a broad class of Signal Temporal Logic (STL) constraints during execution of a pretrained robotics foundation model without modifying its parameters. At each decision step, the method computes a minimally modified action distribution that satisfies a hard STL feasibility constraint by reasoning over the remaining horizon using forward dynamics propagation. We validate the proposed framework in simulation using a state-of-the-art robotics foundation model across multiple environments and complex specifications.

</details>


### [223] [A Single-Fiber Optical Frequency Domain Reflectometry (OFDR)-Based Shape Sensing of Concentric Tube Steerable Drilling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17990)
*Yash Kulkarni,Mobina Tavangarifard,Daniyal Maroufi,Mohsen Khadem,Justin E. Bird,Jeffrey H. Siewerdsen,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于OFDR的同心管导向钻孔机器人形状感知新方法，通过集成OFDR光纤与镍钛合金线制作形状感知组件，在钻孔过程中实现连续应变测量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FBG方法存在局限性，需要开发具有更高空间分辨率和连续测量能力的形状感知技术，以改进同心管导向钻孔机器人的导航精度和安全性

Method: 集成单根OFDR光纤与扁平镍钛合金线制作形状感知组件，将其置于柔性钻孔器械内部通道中，利用预成形的镍钛管引导，钻孔器械作为保护鞘，避免传统光学传感器需要表面粘附的问题

Result: 在自由弯曲条件和合成骨骼模型中沿J形轨迹钻孔测试表明，该方法具有准确可靠的形状感知能力，验证了集成策略的可行性和鲁棒性

Conclusion: 基于OFDR的形状感知方法为同心管导向钻孔机器人提供了有效的形状监测解决方案，消除了传统光学传感器表面集成的需求，提高了系统实用性和可靠性

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel shape-sensing approach for Concentric Tube Steerable Drilling Robots (CT-SDRs) based on Optical Frequency Domain Reflectometry (OFDR). Unlike traditional FBG-based methods, OFDR enables continuous strain measurement along the entire fiber length with enhanced spatial resolution. In the proposed method, a Shape Sensing Assembly (SSA) is first fabricated by integrating a single OFDR fiber with a flat NiTi wire. The calibrated SSA is then routed through and housed within the internal channel of a flexible drilling instrument, which is guided by the pre-shaped NiTi tube of the CT-SDR. In this configuration, the drilling instrument serves as a protective sheath for the SSA during drilling, eliminating the need for integration or adhesion to the instrument surface that is typical of conventional optical sensor approaches. The performance of the proposed SSA, integrated within the cannulated CT-SDR, was thoroughly evaluated under free-bending conditions and during drilling along multiple J-shaped trajectories in synthetic Sawbones phantoms. Results demonstrate accurate and reliable shape-sensing capability, confirming the feasibility and robustness of this integration strategy.

</details>
